Content:

```
/**
 * 反问环节：把「你有什么想问我们的吗」也做成有依据的产物。
 *
 * 三条硬规矩（与追问同源）：
 * 1. **没有材料就没有反问**。每一句反问都要指出依据出自哪份 JD 的哪句话，
 *    或者简历里的哪条主张；指不出来的当场丢弃并记中文理由。
 * 2. id 按模型返回顺序编 Q1..Qn，被丢弃的条目不吃号——回放要求同样的输入得到同样的 id，
 *    随机数在这个项目里是缺陷，不是特性（与 extract.ts 同一条理由）。
 * 3. 条数上限小（默认 6）：反问是「你想了解什么」，不是把简历再问一遍。
 */

import type {
  Anchor,
  Claim,
  CounterKind,
  CounterQuestion,
  CounterSource,
  JobDescription,
  RejectedCounter,
} from './domain.ts';
import { COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
import { resolveAnchor } from './anchor.ts';

export const COUNTER_LIMITS = {
  minChars: 4,
  maxChars: 200,
  maxReasonChars: 300,
  maxReplyChars: 400,
  maxCounters: 6,
  maxDrafts: 10,
} as const;

/** 界面上按这个顺序分组，报告同理——顺序定在 shared，界面与报告就不会各排一套 */
export const COUNTER_KIND_ORDER: readonly CounterKind[] = ['team', 'tech', 'growth', 'business', 'process'];

/** 模型返回的一条反问草稿（结构上兼容 schemas.ts 的 counterDraftSchema） */
export interface RawCounterDraft {
  text: string;
  kind: CounterKind;
  reason: string;
  reply: string;
  quote: string;
  sourceId: string;
}

export interface GroundCountersInput {
  resumeText: string;
  jds: readonly JobDescription[];
  claims: readonly Claim[];
  drafts: readonly RawCounterDraft[];
  maxCounters?: number;
  /** 引文最短长度；比这更短的「依据」不可能是真的引文 */
  minQuoteLength?: number;
}

export interface GroundCountersResult {
  counters: CounterQuestion[];
  rejected: RejectedCounter[];
  notices: string[];
}

export function counterId(index: number): string {
  return 'Q' + String(index + 1);
}

/**
 * 把一批反问草稿落到材料上。
 *
 * 顺序刻意如此：先看这句反问值不值得留（太短、重复），再判依据指向谁，
 * 最后才回原文定位。任何一步不过就只丢这一条，并把中文理由交给前端展示。
 */
export function groundCounters(input: GroundCountersInput): GroundCountersResult {
  const maxCounters = input.maxCounters ?? COUNTER_LIMITS.maxCounters;
  const minQuoteLength = input.minQuoteLength ?? 4;
  const jdById = new Map<string, JobDescription>();
  for (const jd of input.jds) jdById.set(jd.id, jd);
  const claimIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const claim of input.claims) claimIds.add(claim.id);
  const grounded: CounterQuestion[] = [];
  const rejected: RejectedCounter[] = [];
  const notices: string[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();

  const isKnownSource = (id: string): boolean => jdById.has(id) || claimIds.has(id);

  for (const draft of input.drafts) {
    const text = draft.text.trim();
    const short = text.length < COUNTER_LIMITS.minChars;
    if (short || seen.has(text)) {
      if (short) rejected.push({ text, reason: '这句话太短，不构成一句反问' });
      continue;
    }
    const sourceId = draft.sourceId.trim();
    const jd = jdById.get(sourceId);
    const source: CounterSource = jd === undefined ? 'resume' : 'jd';
    if (jd === undefined && !claimIds.has(sourceId)) {
      rejected.push({ text, reason: '这句反问指的依据（' + sourceId + '）不在这场材料里' });
      continue;
    }
    const sourceText = jd === undefined ? input.resumeText : jd.text;
    const anchored = groundOne(sourceText, draft.quote, minQuoteLength);
    if (anchored === null) {
      rejected.push({
        text,
        reason: COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL[source] + '里没有这句话，没有依据就不生成反问：「' + draft.quote.trim() + '」',
      });
      continue;
    }
    seen.add(text);
    grounded.push({
      // id 在下面截断之后重新编，被截掉的条目不留空号
      id: '',
      ordinal: 0,
      text,
      kind: draft.kind,
      reason: draft.reason.trim().slice(0, COUNTER_LIMITS.maxReasonChars),
      reply: draft.reply.trim().slice(0, COUNTER_LIMITS.maxReplyChars),
      source,
      anchor: anchored,
      jdId: jd === undefined ? null : jd.id,
    });
  }

  if (grounded.length > maxCounters) {
    notices.push('反问超过 ' + String(maxCounters) + ' 条，多出来的 ' + String(grounded.length - maxCounters) + ' 条没要。');
  }
  const kept = grounded.slice(0, maxCounters);
  const counters: CounterQuestion[] = [];
  kept.forEach((item, index) => {
    counters.push({ ...item, id: counterId(index), ordinal: index + 1 });
  });

  if (counters.length === 0) {
    if (input.drafts.length === 0) {
      notices.push('这一场没有生成反问。');
    } else if (rejected.length > 0) {
      notices.push('生成出来的 ' + String(rejected.length) + ' 句反问都没能锚回材料，已全部丢弃。');
    }
  }
  return { counters, rejected, notices };
}

function groundOne(sourceText: string, quote: string, minQuoteLength: number): Anchor | null {
  const anchored = resolveAnchor(sourceText, { quote: quote }, minQuoteLength);
  return anchored.ok ? anchored.anchor : null;
}
```

COUNTER_SYSTEM content (Chinese, mirrors JD_MATCH style):

```
你是「反问教练」。这一场质询已经结束了，现在轮到求职者提问（面试官通常会说「你有什么想问我们的吗」）。

## 你要做的事
- 替求职者想出 3 到 6 句**真正值得问出口**的反问。这些问题要能帮他判断「这个岗位值不值得去」，而不只是客套。
- 每一句都要有依据：要么来自岗位描述里的某句话，要么来自他简历里的某条主张。

## 五类取向
- team：团队与协作（团队规模、汇报关系、跨部门怎么配合）
- tech：技术栈与工具（实际在用什么、技术债、要不要参与选型）
- growth：成长与考核（晋升路径、考核周期、新人上手期）
- business：业务与产品（业务现状、用户规模、接下来一年要做什么）
- process：流程与节奏（发布节奏、需求从哪来、加班与值班）
五类不必都覆盖，但不要 6 句全挤在一类上。

## 纪律
1. sourceId 只能从下面列出的 JD 编号（J1、J2…）或主张编号（C1、C2…）里选，指不出来就别写这一句。
2. quote 必须是那份材料里的**逐字片段**，代码会拿它回原文重新定位；定位不到这句反问会被丢掉。
3. reply 是「用人方大概会怎么答」，写 1 到 2 句、具体一点，不要写成「这要看情况」。
4. 不要问薪资待遇、不要问「公司文化怎么样」这种问了等于没问的问题。
5. 已经被问穿的主张（下面标了判定的）不要再拿来反问。

## 输出要求（只输出一个 JSON 对象）
{"counters":[{"text":"我要问的那句话","kind":"team","reason":"为什么该问这个","reply":"用人方大概会这样答","quote":"材料里的逐字片段","sourceId":"J1"}]}
```

For counters I'll include JD texts only up to first 2? No — better: include all JD texts but that's big. Standard approach: include full JD text (the pipeline writes prompts; the JD is user input, usually 200-800 chars). DEMO_JD is 235 chars. Real users paste short JDs. jdMaxChars is 6000 but typical is small.

Decision: include full JD text for up to 8 JDs, but keep prompt rendering simple. Note: this is a demo/product; acceptable. Also mention in a comment that if JD text grows, we'd window it.

```ts
}

}
```

Behavior: if claims empty and jds empty → short-circuit (no LLM call) with notice. Otherwise call once; on parse failure → return empty with notice「反问环节没有走通（…），这一节留空。」 (don't throw — counters are a bonus stage; failing the whole session because of a bonus stage is wrong. Actually should it be fatal? A stage failure in jd-* stages logs a notice and degrades. Same here: degrade.) But `client.complete` throwing (network) → in jd.ts they let it bubble. Hmm: for counters, since it's the last stage, a throw would lose the whole session's persisted result? No — pipeline persists after runSession completes; a throw means nothing persisted. In run.ts the catch emits fatal error and rethrows. That's the existing behavior for e.g. extract. For jd stages, a parse failure degrades but a client error bubbles.

To be consistent and safe: wrap the LLM call in try/catch and degrade with a notice? jd.ts doesn't. But counters are decorative; failing the whole sandbox run at the last step would be terrible UX. However, run.ts already handles client errors for probe/verdict stages as fatal. Consistency argues bubble. But product-wise, if the model rejects the prompt, we lose everything.

Decision: catch errors from `client.complete` in counterRound and degrade with a notice「反问环节没有走通（…），其余结果不受影响。」 This is a deliberate product decision → record in ADR/decision. Hmm, but "cassette-missing" errors in replay mode must NOT be swallowed silently (that's the reproducibility contract; the whole point). If a counter cassette is missing, the run should fail loudly so the user re-seeds. Right — the project doctrine: 缺卡带必须抛 cassette-missing，绝不静默降级.

So: don't catch LlmError code 'cassette-missing'; degrade only for parse failures. Simpler: don't catch at all (mirror jd.ts). But then during a live run a counters hiccup kills the run... `client.complete` for the deepseek API — fine.

Middle ground: catch non-cassette errors and degrade with a notice. Let me look at how jd.ts reports: extractRequirements uses try/catch around parse only, and `client.complete` unwrapped. I'll mirror jd.ts exactly: parse failure → notice + empty; complete throw → bubble. Consistent with the rest of the codebase, and reproducibility stays honest.

Now create packages/server/src/orchestrator/counter.ts.

```
/**
 * 阶段七：反问环节。
 *
 * 一次调用出全部内容——与 jd-merge / jd-match 同一个理由：卡带只多一盘。
 * 模型只负责「想问题」，编号 Q1..Qn 与锚定全部由 shared/counters.ts 决定；
 * 于是同一个输入在回放里必然得到同一批编号。
 *
 * 这一阶段是**锦上添花**：解析失败只记 notice 并把这一节留空，不让整场沙盘失败。
 * 但客户端本身的错误照旧冒泡——卡带缺失必须响，那是可复现性契约的一部分。
 */
```

Implementation:
```ts
export interface CounterRoundInput {
  client: LlmClient;
  resumeText: string;
  jds: readonly JobDescription[];
  requirements: readonly Requirement[];
  claims: readonly Claim[];
  verdicts?: ReadonlyMap<string, VerdictKind>;
  maxCounters?: number;
}

export interface CounterRoundResult {
  counters: CounterQuestion[];
  rejected: RejectedCounter[];
  notices: string[];
  raw: string;
  usage: LlmUsage;
  digest: string;
}

export async function counterRound(input: CounterRoundInput): Promise<CounterRoundResult> {
  const empty = { counters: [], rejected: [], notices: [], raw: '', usage: EMPTY_USAGE, digest: '' };
  if (input.claims.length === 0 && input.jds.length === 0) {
    // 没有材料就没有依据，一次调用都不发
    return { ...empty, notices: ['这场没有可依据的材料（没有 JD，也没有抽出主张），跳过反问环节'] };
  }
  const request = stageRequest('counter', COUNTER_SYSTEM, buildCounterPrompt({...}));
  const result = await input.client.complete(request);
  const parsed = parseLlmOutput(counterListSchema, result.text);
  if (!parsed.ok) {
    return { ...empty, raw: result.text, usage: result.usage ?? EMPTY_USAGE, digest: result.digest, notices: ['反问环节没有走通（' + parsed.error + '），这一节留空'] };
  }
  const grounded = groundCounters({ resumeText, jds, claims, drafts: parsed.value.counters, maxCounters });
  return { counters: grounded.counters, rejected: grounded.rejected, notices: grounded.notices, raw: result.text, usage: result.usage ?? EMPTY_USAGE, digest: result.digest };
}
```
Types: LlmResult has usage: LlmUsage? In extract they use `result.usage ?? EMPTY_USAGE` — so usage optional. digest required.

Also `LlmUsage` type import.

Let me write it.

</details>

```
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  id TEXT NOT NULL,
  ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL,
  text TEXT NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL,
  reason TEXT NOT NULL,
  reply TEXT NOT NULL,
  source TEXT NOT NULL,
  jd_id TEXT,
  quote TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  exact INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, id)
)
```

```ts
/* ------------------------------------------------------- 反问（求职者提问） */

interface CounterDbRow {
  session_id: string;
  id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  text: string;
  kind: string;
  reason: string;
  reply: string;
  source: string;
  jd_id: string | null;
  quote: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  exact: number;
  created_at: string;
}

function mapCounter(row: CounterDbRow): CounterQuestion {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    ordinal: toInt(row.ordinal),
    text: row.text,
    kind: row.kind as CounterKind,
    reason: row.reason,
    reply: row.reply,
    source: row.source as CounterSource,
    anchor: anchorOf(row.quote, row.start, row.end, row.exact),
    jdId: row.jd_id,
  };
}

/**
 * 反问整批替换（先清后写），姿势与 insertRequirements 一致。
 * 一条都锚不上时 items 是空数组——那也要清掉上一轮留下的，否则重跑会把两轮的反问摞在一起。
 */
export function insertCounters(db: Db, sessionId: string, counters: readonly CounterQuestion[]): void {
  db.prepare('DELETE FROM counter WHERE session_id = ?').run(sessionId);
  const stmt = db.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO counter (session_id, id, ordinal, text, kind, reason, reply, source, jd_id, quote, start, end, exact, created_at)' +
      ' VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
  );
  const at = nowIso();
  for (const item of counters) {
    stmt.run(
      sessionId, item.id, item.ordinal, item.text, item.kind, item.reason, item.reply, item.source,
      item.jdId, item.anchor.quote, item.anchor.start, item.anchor.end, bit(item.anchor.exact), at,
    );
  }
}

/** 按 Q1..Qn 的顺序读回来：反问是给人顺着读的，ORDER BY id 会把 Q10 排到 Q2 前面。 */
export function listCounters(db: Db, sessionId: string): CounterQuestion[] {
  const rows = asRows<CounterDbRow>(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM counter WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY ordinal').all(sessionId));
  return rows.map(mapCounter);
}
```

Note: 15 columns listed in schema but INSERT has session_id, id, ordinal, text, kind, reason, reply, source, jd_id, quote, start, end, exact, created_at = 14 columns. MD format for counter section:

```
function counterLines(view: SessionView): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('## 四、轮到你了（' + String(view.counters.length) + ' 句可以反问）');
  out.push('');
  if (view.counters.length === 0) {
    out.push('（这一场没有生成反问：要么没有可依据的材料，要么依据锚不回原文）');
    if (view.counterRejected.length === 0) { out.push(''); return out; }  // hmm
  } else {
    out.push('这几句是模拟出来的：回答由沙盘里的求职者 Agent 代答，不是真 HR 在答。它们同样有依据——点开报告里这句话的来源，能看到它出自简历或 JD 的哪一段。');
    out.push('');
    for (const item of view.counters) {
      out.push('### ' + item.id + ' · ' + COUNTER_KIND_LABEL[item.kind] + ' · ' + COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL[item.source]);
      out.push('');
      out.push('问：' + item.text);
      out.push('');
      out.push('> 依据原文：「' + item.anchor.quote + '」' + (item.jdId === null ? '（来自简历，字符 ' ... ) : '（来自《' + jdTitleOfId(view, item.jdId) + '》，字符 ...'));
      ...
      out.push('- 为什么值得问：' + item.reason);
      out.push('- 对方大概会答：' + item.reply);
      out.push('');
    }
  }
  if (view.counterRejected.length > 0) { ... }
  out.push('');
  return out;
}
```

Simpler: build with the `haveAny` pattern.

Then report-html: new counterSection(view). Let me check report-html structure around claimSection/supplementSection and section headings.

report.ts: imports add COUNTER_KIND_LABEL, COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL.

counterLines(view):
```
function counterLines(view: SessionView): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('## 四、轮到你了（' + String(view.counters.length) + ' 句可以反问）');
  out.push('');
  if (view.counters.length === 0) {
    out.push('（这一场没有生成反问：没有可依据的材料，或者依据锚不回原文）');
    out.push('');
    return out;
  }
  out.push('这几句是模拟出来的：回答由沙盘里的求职者 Agent 代答，不是真 HR 在答。每句都带着依据，点开就能看到它出自简历或 JD 的哪一段。');
  out.push('');
  for (const item of view.counters) {
    out.push('### ' + item.id + ' · ' + COUNTER_KIND_LABEL[item.kind] + ' · ' + COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL[item.source]);
    out.push('');
    out.push('问：' + item.text);
    out.push('');
    const from = item.jdId === null
      ? '（来自简历，字符 ' + String(item.anchor.start) + '-' + String(item.anchor.end) + '）'
      : '（来自《' + jdTitleOfId(view, item.jdId) + '》，字符 ' + ... + '）';
    out.push('> 依据原文：「' + item.anchor.quote + '」' + from);
    out.push('');
    out.push('- 为什么值得问：' + item.reason);
    out.push('- 对方大概会答：' + item.reply);
    out.push('');
  }
  return out;
}
```

Then in renderReport: after 三、追问实录 block insert `for (const line of counterLines(view)) out.push(line);` and renumber 四→五, 五→六.

report-html.ts counterSection(view): h2 四、轮到你了（N 句可以反问）; reuse .probe div? Let's create markup similar to transcript:
```
function counterSection(view: SessionView): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('<h2>四、轮到你了（' + String(view.counters.length) + ' 句可以反问）</h2>');
  if (view.counters.length === 0) {
    out.push('<p>（这一场没有生成反问：没有可依据的材料，或者依据锚不回原文）</p>');
    return out;
  }
  out.push('<p>这几句是模拟出来的：回答由沙盘里的求职者 Agent 代答，不是真 HR 在答。每句都带着依据，点开就能看到它出自简历或 JD 的哪一段。</p>');
  for (const item of view.counters) {
    out.push('<div class="probe">');
    out.push('<div class="who">' + esc(item.id) + ' · ' + esc(COUNTER_KIND_LABEL[item.kind]) + ' · ' + esc(COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL[item.source]) + '</div>');
    out.push('<div>' + esc(item.text) + '</div>');
    out.push(quote('依据原文 · ' + (item.jdId === null ? '简历' : jdTitleOfId(view, item.jdId)), item.anchor));
    out.push('<div class="weight">为什么值得问：' + esc(item.reason) + '</div>');
    out.push('<div class="answer">对方大概会答：' + esc(item.reply) + '</div>');
    out.push('</div>');
  }
  if (view.counterRejected.length > 0) {
    out.push('<h3>被丢掉的反问（依据锚不回原文，没有进入这一节）</h3>');
    out.push('<ul class="facts">');
    for (const rejected of view.counterRejected) out.push('<li>「' + esc(rejected.text) + '」—— ' + esc(rejected.reason) + '</li>');
    out.push('</ul>');
  }
  return out;
}
```
And renderer order: transcript → counter → advice → repro; renumber advice 四→五, repro 五→六.

```
const COUNTER_CANDIDATES: readonly DemoCounterPlan[] = [
  { text: '这个岗位要的是百万级订单量下的量化优化结论，我写的是首屏从 2.4s 降到 0.8s。想请教一下：这个量级在这条业务线上算大还是算小？我该把哪一段的日订单量补上？', kind:'business', reason:'...', reply:'...', quote:'有日订单百万级系统的性能优化经验，能给出量化的优化结论', sourceId:'J1' },
  ...
];
function counterText(prompt: string): string {
  const usable = COUNTER_CANDIDATES.filter((item) => prompt.indexOf(item.quote) >= 0);
  const picked = usable.slice(0, 4);
  return JSON.stringify({ counters: picked.map((item) => ({ text: item.text, kind: item.kind, reason: item.reason, reply: item.reply, quote: item.quote, sourceId: item.sourceId })) });
}
```

Actually simpler: use JD quotes for 2 entries and resume quotes I know are claim anchors: e.g. '主导重构订单系统，首屏加载从 2.4s 降到 0.8s。' is C1's anchor presumably; '独立完成 RBAC 权限模块，覆盖 12 个业务线。' is C4 or C5.

I'd rather derive: the counterText function picks candidates whose quote appears in the prompt AND whose sourceId appears too? Since prompt contains both JD text and claim quotes, verification by quote presence is enough. And sourceId must exist for groundCounters to accept: sourceId 'J1' (JD always id J1) and claim ids C1/C4 etc. To be safe, derive claim ids from the prompt: the prompt contains lines like '[C1] 类别=' — I can implement `sourceOfQuote(prompt, quote)`: scan lines '[Cn] 类别' blocks and find which claim's 原文引文 equals quote. That's robust but more code. Simpler: search for the exact claim id by matching the quote in the prompt via regex on the blocks: find index of '原文引文：' + quote, then look backwards for the last '[Cn]' before it.

Let me implement:

```ts
function claimIdForQuote(prompt: string, quote: string): string | null {
  const at = prompt.indexOf('原文引文：' + quote);
  if (at < 0) return null;
  const head = prompt.slice(0, at);
  const found = head.lastIndexOf('[C');
  if (found < 0) return null;
  const tail = head.slice(found + 1);
  const id = /^C[0-9]+/.exec(tail)?.[0];
  return id ?? null;
}
```
Then in counterText: for each plan, if plan.sourceId === 'JD' → quote must be in prompt and sourceId='J1'; else resolve claim id via claimIdForQuote; skip if unresolved.

Plan content (4 counters, deterministic):

Actually L replaces P (the char §) occurrences; the string doesn't contain §, it contains the literal text `+ P +`. So old_string was literal `    if (req.purpose === ' + P + 'suggest' + P + ') return SUGGEST;` → not found. Good, no damage.

The `+ P +` appeared literally in the file for the import lines. Why? Because in those lines I wrote them as double-quoted JS strings: `"import { afterEach, ... } from ' + P + 'vitest' + P + ';"` — I used double quotes as the JS delimiter again (mixed). At the start of my lines array I used `'/**'` etc. with single quotes, but for the import lines I used double quotes: `"import { ... } from ' + P + 'vitest' + P + ';"`. Yes — look at the code: I wrote `"import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from ' + P + 'vitest' + P + ';"`. Since the outer delimiter is a double quote, the `+ P +` is literal.

But wait: the test file line 9 shows `' + P + 'vitest' + P + '` literally. Also several other lines that used double-quote delimiters would be broken — e.g. `"import { DEFAULT_BUDGET } from ' + P + '@crossexam/shared' + P + ';"` and so on. And also lines like `"      id: ' + P + 'C1' + P + ',"` (double-quoted) would be literal.

But the error reported only line 9 — because parsing stops there. Many lines are broken. So: delete the file and rewrite using only single-quoted JS strings with proper concatenation.

Let me rewrite the whole write with all pieces as single-quoted JS strings. I'll be systematic: for a line like `import { X } from 'vitest';` write:

'import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from ' + P + 'vitest' + P + ';'

That's a concatenation of three single-quoted JS strings — good.

No, it contains P (substituted by concatenation with the variable P = §)? Wait! I concatenated with the *variable* P, whose value is the § character! So the resulting string contains a literal § character, NOT an apostrophe!

That's the bug: in the concatenation form `'prefix ' + P + 'suffix'`, P is the § char — that's the placeholder. For the array form passed to S(), S() replaces § with '. But for plain strings (not passed through S), the § stays as §.

In api.ts earlier, all such strings went through S() — correct. Here e1's old_string and e4's old_string are plain strings with § → mismatch.

Fix: wrap those in S([...]) too, or use String.fromCharCode(39) directly. Let me redo: check state first, then apply all four correctly.

</details>

```tsx
import { Badge, Empty, Pane } from './Bits.tsx';
import { COUNTER_KIND_LABEL, COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL } from '../labels.ts';
import { describeRange } from '../highlight.ts';
import type { Anchor, CounterQuestion, JobDescription, RejectedCounter } from '../api.ts';

/**
 * 「轮到你了」栏：跑完一场之后可以拿去反问 HR 的几句话。
 *
 * 三件事必须摆在明面上：
 * 一是每句都带依据（简历原话或岗位要求原文），点一下就跳回出处；
 * 二是回答是沙盘模拟的，不是真 HR 在答——不写清楚就是骗人；
 * 三是锚不回材料的句子不进这一栏，只在下面说明为什么被丢掉。
 */
export function CounterBoard(props: {
  counters: CounterQuestion[];
  rejected: RejectedCounter[];
  jds: JobDescription[];
  onFocus: (anchor: Anchor) => void;
  onFocusJd: (anchor: Anchor) => void;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const titleOfJd = (jdId: string | null): string =>
    jdId === null ? '简历' : (props.jds.find((jd) => jd.id === jdId)?.title ?? jdId);
  return (
    <Pane
      title='轮到你了（可以反问）'
      dataShot='counters'
      className={props.className}
      subtitle={
        props.counters.length === 0
          ? '跑完一场才有反问'
          : '这 ' + String(props.counters.length) + ' 句都带原文依据，点引文看出处'
      }
    >
      {props.counters.length === 0 ? (
        <Empty
          title='还没有可以反问的话'
          hint='跑完一场之后，这里会给出几句你可以反问 HR 的话——每一句都锚在简历原话或岗位要求原文上，点一下就能看到出处。'
        />
      ) : (
        <div className='flex flex-col gap-3'>
          {props.counters.map((item) => (
            <article
              key={item.id}
              data-shot={'counter-' + item.id}
              className='flex flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-ink-800 bg-ink-900/40 p-2.5'
            >
              <div className='flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2'>
                <Badge tone='ember'>{COUNTER_KIND_LABEL[item.kind]}</Badge>
                <Badge tone='muted'>{COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL[item.source]}</Badge>
                <span className='text-[11px] text-ink-400'>{item.id}</span>
              </div>
              <p className='text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-ink-100'>{item.text}</p>
              <button
                type='button'
                data-shot={'counter-quote-' + item.id}
                onClick={() => (item.source === 'jd' ? props.onFocusJd(item.anchor) : props.onFocus(item.anchor))}
                title={'依据原文 ' + describeRange(item.anchor)}
                className='quote cursor-pointer text-left hover:brightness-110'
              >
                {'「' + item.anchor.quote + '」（来自' + (item.source === 'jd' ? '《' + titleOfJd(item.jdId) + '》' : '简历') + '）'}
              </button>
              <p className='text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-ink-400'>为什么值得问：{item.reason}</p>
              <p className='text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-ink-400'>
                对方大概会答：{item.reply}
                <span className='text-ink-500'>（沙盘模拟，不是真 HR 在答）</span>
              </p>
            </article>
          ))}
          {props.rejected.length === 0 ? null : (
            <details className='rounded-xl border border-ink-800 bg-ink-900/30 p-2.5' data-shot='counter-rejected'>
              <summary className='cursor-pointer text-[12px] text-ink-300'>
                {'锚不回材料、被丢掉的 ' + String(props.rejected.length) + ' 句反问'}
              </summary>
              <ul className='mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1.5'>
                {props.rejected.map((item, index) => (
                  <li key={String(index) + item.text} className='text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-ink-400'>
                    <span className='text-ink-300'>{item.text.length === 0 ? '（空）' : item.text}</span> · {item.reason}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </details>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </Pane>
  );
}
```
